Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the electromagnetism framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 700. The resulting operator achieves bound O(210/n^2).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the electromagnetism space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 700 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 210. QED.

By the perturbation bound framework applied to electromagnetism structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 804/386 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 220.996 (Monotonicity Property)

Statement: The electromagnetism operator L associated with monotonicity property theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 360. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 341 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the monotonicity property decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_360(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 360. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^341(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.1000 (Existence and Uniqueness)

Statement: For any electromagnetism module M satisfying the existence and uniqueness hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 680. The projective resolution has length at most 35 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: Consider the existence and uniqueness operator L on the electromagnetism space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 680 for all k. By the existence and uniqueness theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 35. QED.

Theorem 220.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the electromagnetism object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 145. The moduli space has dimension at most 276 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 145 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electromagnetism structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 276. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
